### AC 3.13 Daily quiz (DAILY-001, #119)

**API**

- **AC 3.13.1** [Anyone] **Given** I call `GET /api/daily-quiz`, **then** I receive today's UTC date (`yyyy-MM-dd`) and one global set of at most 5 public items, identical for every caller that day. The set is chosen the first time the day is read and then stored with a header row, so concurrent first reads produce one set and a day with nothing eligible stays empty without being recomputed (it is not refilled later, even if content becomes eligible that day). Signed-in callers also receive their completion for the day (or null) and their streak; anonymous callers receive null for both.
- **AC 3.13.2** [System] **Given** a day's set is created, **then** eligible items are public, in a public category, have at least one incorrect answer, are not flagged **check answer key** by the question quality report (ANALYTICS-009, default 30-day window), and are not part of any other stored daily set within 30 days before or after that date; eligible items are ordered by the first 8 bytes of SHA-256(`"yyyy-MM-dd:" + itemId`) ascending (ties by item id) and the first 5 are taken. The day boundary is UTC midnight. "Has at least one incorrect answer" means at least one **playable** distractor per **AC 3.6.17** (a blank-only or correct-answer-equivalent list is not eligible), so the client total always equals the stored set size (#250).
- **AC 3.13.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /api/daily-quiz/completions` with `{ date, correct, total }`, **when** the date is today or yesterday (UTC, so a run that crosses midnight still counts), that day's set has items, and `total` equals its number of active items, **then** my first completion for that date is stored (201) and later calls return the stored one unchanged (200, `created=false`); both return my streak. Otherwise the API returns 400 and stores nothing; anonymous callers get 401.
- **AC 3.13.4** [Any user] **Given** completion dates, **then** the streak is the number of consecutive UTC days with a completion ending today, or ending yesterday when today is not completed yet. One completed daily quiz is enough for a streak day.
- **AC 3.13.5** [Admin] **Given** I call `GET /api/admin/daily-quiz?date=yyyy-MM-dd` (today to 7 days ahead, default today; the set is created if missing) or `POST /api/admin/daily-quiz/{date}/items/{itemId}/exclude`, **then** I see the day's active and removed items, and removing an active item marks it excluded for that date and fills its slot with the next eligible item in seed order; an item not active in that day's set returns 404, a simultaneous change to the same day by another admin 409, a date outside the window 400, and non-admins 403. Completions already recorded for that date keep their streak credit.

**UI**

- **AC 3.13.6** [Anyone] **Given** I open `/daily`, **then** I can answer today's questions with the same answer locking, results, and **Review mistakes** behaviour as a quiz run (AC 3.6.8-3.6.11), without per-item URLs. The first completed run of the day counts: signed in, the SPA posts the completion (AC 3.13.3) and, when it was newly stored, saves the run as a study session with no source so its misses join my review queue; anonymous, the score is stored by date in `localStorage` `quizymode.daily.v1` (version 1, at most 60 dates, first score per date wins, corrupt data ignored, separate from `quizymode.guest.v1` so **Clear guest history** keeps its meaning) and the page notes that the streak is saved in this browser only. Anonymous daily runs are not added to guest history.
- **AC 3.13.7** [Anyone] **Given** today's daily quiz is already counted for me, **when** I open `/daily`, **then** I see **Done for today** with my score and a **Practice again** action; practice runs never change the stored score or streak. Loading, empty ("There is no daily quiz today"), save-failure, and load-error (with **Try again**) states are shown.
- **AC 3.13.8** [Admin] **Given** I open `/admin/daily-quiz` (linked from the admin dashboard), **then** I can pick a UTC day from today to 7 days ahead, see its questions with category and a link to the item, and **Remove** one; the list then shows the replacement and a **Removed for this day** list.
- **AC 3.13.9** [Anyone] **Given** I finish the daily quiz (counted or practice run), **when** I share, **then** the text is line 1 `Quizymode Daily {date} {n}/{t}`, line 2 one U+1F7E9 (correct) or U+1F7E5 (incorrect) per question in roster order with no separators, and line 3 `{origin}/daily?utm_source=share&utm_medium=share&utm_campaign=quiz_result&utm_content=daily`. No question text is included, and the QR code is available (**AC 3.19**).

---

### AC 3.14 Review queue (REVIEW-001, #117)

**API**

- **AC 3.14.1** [Authenticated] **Given** a newly saved live quiz run (AC 3.6.14) has missed items that exist, **then** each is (re)scheduled in my review queue at the first interval, due one day after the run was saved (server time), including items already queued at a later interval. Items that are not quiz-playable per **AC 3.6.17** are skipped and never queued. The queue is private to me, keyed by item, and saved after the study session so a queue conflict never loses the session.
- **AC 3.14.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `GET /api/review-queue`, **then** I receive at most 20 due items (due time at or before now), oldest due first, plus my due count, total count, and the next future due time. Queued items that were deleted or are private to someone else are removed from my queue silently; items changed since they were queued (their `UpdatedAt`, which admin moderation or recategorization also sets) restart at the first interval and are due the next day, like a fresh miss. `GET /api/review-queue/summary` returns only the counts and next due time for available items. Queued items that are not quiz-playable per **AC 3.6.17** are removed silently and are never served or counted in the due or total counts (#250). They are also never enqueued (**AC 3.14.1**, **AC 3.14.5**, #251).
- **AC 3.14.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /api/review-queue/{itemId}/answers` with `{ isCorrect }` for a due item, **then** a correct answer advances it one interval (1, 3, 7, 14, 30 days) and a correct answer at 30 days removes it (`graduated=true`); an incorrect answer resets it to 1 day. An entry that is not due yet is left unchanged (`applied=false`), so repeated submits are safe; an item not in my queue returns 404; anonymous callers get 401. Correct answers in ordinary quizzes do not change the queue.

**UI**

- **AC 3.14.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I open `/review` (sign-in required), **then** the current due items load fresh on every visit and stay fixed while I answer (no refetch on window focus); each first answer is saved as I choose it, and a live summary shows how many moved to a longer interval, came back for tomorrow, or are done. When more items are due than shown, the page says so. A failed save is reported and the item stays due. With nothing due, the page shows how many are scheduled and when the next is due (or how the queue fills), with links to the daily quiz and categories. Review runs report quiz analytics with context type `review` and are not saved as study sessions.

**Flashcard self-grading (#251)**

- **AC 3.14.5** [Authenticated] `POST /api/review-queue/{itemId}` with no body, on an item I can see outside any collection (public, or my own private item) that is quiz-playable per **AC 3.6.17**:
  - It queues the item at the first interval, due one day after the call (server time). This also applies when the item is already queued at a later interval, which resets it like a quiz miss (**AC 3.14.1**).
  - Repeat calls keep exactly one entry.
  - It returns 200 with `itemId`, `intervalDays=1` and `dueAt` (UTC).
  - A missing item, a private item owned by someone else (even one visible through a shared collection) and an item that is not quiz-playable all return 404 with the same body and create no entry.
  - Anonymous callers get 401.
  - More than 300 calls by the same user in 10 minutes returns 429 with the message "Too many Review queue updates. Please slow down."; the limit is per user.
- **AC 3.14.6** [Anyone] In Flashcards (all scopes), after I reveal a card, a group labelled "How well did you know this?" shows **Got it** then **Still learning**. It is not nested in the flip buttons and is hidden on the front face.
  - The chosen grade has `aria-pressed=true` and a check icon. Choosing the other grade replaces it, and choosing the pressed one does nothing.
  - The shown grade follows the card id.
  - Grading never advances the card, and Next works without grading.
  - **Got it** never calls the review-queue API and never removes or promotes a queue entry, including after Still learning.
- **AC 3.14.7** [Authenticated] **Still learning** adds the card to the session pile at once. If this item has not already been enqueued during this page visit, it calls **AC 3.14.5**. Status messages appear in a persistent `role="status"` line:
  - Success shows "Added to Review", which clears after 3 s or on card change.
  - A network, 5xx or 429 failure shows "Couldn't add to Review. It stays in this session's pile." with **Retry**.
  - A 404 shows "This card can't be added to Review" without Retry.
  - The card stays in the pile in every case.
- **AC 3.14.8** [Anyone] On the last card, Next becomes **Finish**, which is always enabled, and the right arrow key acts as Finish there. Finish shows a summary with a focused heading:
  - The heading is "Deck complete", or "Round complete" in a loop.
  - It shows "{g} got it · {s} still learning · {n} not graded"; counts are per round.
  - **Study N still-learning cards** (singular for 1) runs only those cards, in deck order, with fresh grades: cards left ungraded in a loop round are not carried over. The loop shows "1 of N", has a "Still learning" round label, and uses per-item URLs that start at the first loop card. Loops repeat until none remain, which then shows "All caught up. Nice work."
  - **Restart deck** always starts a new run: new run id, pile cleared, first card.
  - The pile and grades are per run, are not persisted, and are cleared by a reload, by leaving the page or by changing the deck's scope.
  - The stored navigation context, comment navigation, reveal and grade positions, and the switch to Quiz mode always use the full deck, never the loop subset.
- **AC 3.14.9** [Anonymous] Grading works with the in-session pile only and makes no review-queue API call. When at least one card is still learning, the summary shows one `SignupPrompt` with the reason `review-later` offering to keep these cards in a Review queue after creating an account. Nothing is written to guest storage or imported on sign-in.
- **AC 3.14.10** [Anyone] Collection-scope Flashcards show a **Shuffle** switch (`role=switch`, default off). It is saved in `localStorage` `quizymode.flashcards.shuffle.v1`, and if storage fails it works for the session only.
  - With shuffle on, the deck is shuffled once per run, before the first URL sync. The first URL is the shuffled first card.
  - A card in the URL at load (deep link or reload) stays first, and the rest are shuffled.
  - Moving between cards never reshuffles, and a refetch that drops a card keeps the remaining cards in the same order.
  - Toggling starts a new run with the current card first: index 0, pile and grades cleared, new run id. If any card in the run is graded, a confirm dialog appears first, and Cancel changes nothing.
  - Category and whole-bank scopes show no Shuffle switch.
- **AC 3.14.11** [Authenticated] **Given** I finish a review-queue run, **then** the results view shows no **Share result** button and no placeholder (**AC 3.19**).

### AC 3.15 Topic interests (PERSONAL-001, #116)

- **AC 3.15.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `GET /api/users/me/interests`, **then** I receive my interest category slugs in the order I chose them, omitting categories that were deleted or made private. **Given** I call `PUT /api/users/me/interests` with `{ categories }`, **then** at most 10 unique slugs (`^[a-z0-9_-]+$`) of current public categories replace my list in that order (an empty list clears it); unknown or private slugs, duplicates, or more than 10 return 400 and change nothing; anonymous callers get 401. Interests are stored by category id, so renaming a category keeps them.
- **AC 3.15.2** [Anyone] **Given** I call `GET /api/home/popular-categories`, **then** I receive up to 6 public category slugs ranked by completed category quiz runs (Learn runs are not counted, AC 3.17.11) in the last 30 days (then runs started), from the learning report aggregation with bots and internal traffic excluded; only current public categories are returned, never counts, and the result is cached for an hour.
- **AC 3.15.3** [Anyone] **Given** I open the topic picker on `/`, **then** it is a labelled modal dialog listing the bundled home categories as toggle chips (at most 10 selectable; the rest disable at the limit), closing with **Cancel**, the backdrop, or Escape and returning focus. **Save topics** stores the list with the API when I am signed in, or in `localStorage` `quizymode.interests.v1` (version 1) when anonymous; a failed save keeps the dialog open with an error.
- **AC 3.15.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I sign in in a browser holding anonymous topic picks, **when** the once-per-page-session guest claim runs (AC 3.6.16), **then** the picks that match bundled categories are appended after my account's existing interests (account order first, capped at 10), saved once, and the local copy is cleared only after the server accepts; on failure it stays for a later retry. No message is shown.

### AC 3.16 Study keyboard shortcuts (#252)

- **AC 3.16.1** [Anyone] In Quiz, Daily quiz and Review, before the current item is answered, pressing 1-4 or A-D selects that option, with the same scoring, locking, analytics and feedback as a click.
  - Digits on the top row or numpad work with or without Shift, so AZERTY users can use them.
  - A key for an option that does not exist does nothing. Answer keys do nothing once the item is answered, so it is never scored twice.
- **AC 3.16.2** [Anyone] In Quiz, Daily quiz and Review:
  - Left/Right arrows act exactly like the visible Previous and Next buttons and are inert when those buttons are disabled.
  - After answering, Enter opens **View results** if every item is answered, otherwise it acts as **Next** when Next is enabled. The right arrow never opens results.
  - In Daily quiz and Review, focus still moves to the "Question X of N" heading after navigation.
  - On the results, mistake-review and summary views no answer, arrow or Enter shortcut is active.
  - In a started Test (AC 3.18) 1-4 / A-D select and re-select (the last answer counts), Enter and the right arrow only ever mean Next, F toggles the review flag, and no key opens or confirms Submit. Nothing is active on the Test setup card.
- **AC 3.16.3** [Anyone] In Flashcards, Space or Enter flips the card in both directions. Flipping to the answer fires the same reveal as a click.
  - Left/Right arrows go to the previous and next card when those buttons are enabled; on the last card the right arrow is **Finish** (**AC 3.14.8**).
  - Handled keys call preventDefault, so Space does not scroll the page.
- **AC 3.16.4** [Anyone] In Flashcards, after the answer is shown and the Got it / Still learning buttons are visible, 1 = Got it and 2 = Still learning, through the same handlers as the buttons. On the end-of-deck, results and mistake-review screens, only `?` is active.
- **AC 3.16.5** [Anyone] A shortcut is ignored when:
  - focus is in an input, textarea, select, contentEditable, `role=textbox` or `role=combobox`
  - IME composition is in progress (`isComposing` or keyCode 229)
  - the key is an auto-repeat
  - the event was already `defaultPrevented`
  - Ctrl, Alt or Meta is held
  - any `role=dialog` or `aria-modal` element is mounted (comments drawer, collections modal, profile modal, feedback dialog, collection details, share dialog, the shortcuts dialog)
  - Space or Enter is pressed while an **enabled** button, link, `role=button`, `role=switch` or summary has focus. That control activates exactly once.
  - Space or Enter is pressed on a **disabled** focused control: this does not block the shortcut.
  - An arrow key is pressed while a slider, range, listbox, radiogroup or tablist has focus.
- **AC 3.16.6** [Anyone] Every active flashcard and quiz-question screen (StudyShell header and the Daily quiz / Review header) shows a **Keyboard shortcuts** button. It is icon-only below `sm` and labelled "Shortcuts" from `sm` up, has `aria-label="Keyboard shortcuts"`, and is shown on all pointer types.
  - The button, or `?` on any study screen including results and end-of-deck, opens a modal dialog (`role=dialog`, `aria-modal`, labelled by its title).
  - The dialog lists only the bindings active on the current screen. When none apply it shows "No other shortcuts on this screen."
  - Initial focus is on the **Single-key shortcuts** switch, and Tab and Shift+Tab cycle within the dialog.
  - Escape, Close or a backdrop click closes it, and focus returns to the element that had focus when it opened.
- **AC 3.16.7** [Anyone] The **Single-key shortcuts** switch (`role=switch`, `aria-checked`) is on by default.
  - It persists in localStorage `quizymode.study-hotkeys.v1` (values on/off), survives a reload, and syncs across tabs.
  - When it is off, no single-key shortcut fires, including `?`, but the Keyboard shortcuts button still opens the dialog.
  - If storage is unavailable, the switch still works for the current tab, and a status note says the setting could not be saved.
- **AC 3.16.8** [Anyone] The ItemCollectionsModal, UserProfileModal and the "Collection details" overlays on the Flashcards and Quiz pages expose `role=dialog` and `aria-modal=true` with an accessible name. Quiz option buttons expose `aria-keyshortcuts` (for example "1 A").

### AC 3.17 Learn mode (#254)

- **AC 3.17.1** [Anyone] /learn/collections/:collectionId/:slug? and /learn/:category? (with keywords as on Quiz) load the playable multiple-choice items in scope (#250 predicate). A collection uses its full playable roster, shuffled by run, capped at 50, with the caption "Learning 50 of N" when capped. Category/global scopes use "Items to learn" (1-50, default 10), and M is the playable count actually returned. Zero playable items shows "Nothing to learn here yet" with **Open flashcards** and no run. A load error shows a role=alert with **Try again**. (page test)
- **AC 3.17.2** A round holds min(7, unmastered) items: attempted-but-unmastered items first (oldest attempt first), then unseen items in roster order. With 2 or more items, a round never starts with the item that ended the previous one. 1-item rounds work. (reducer: 1, 2, 3, 7, 8 and 50 items)
- **AC 3.17.3** A correct first-ever attempt masters an item. A miss resets the streak, and then 2 consecutive correct answers (one per round) master it. Every attempted unmastered item appears in every later round, and there are never more than 7. The run finishes when all M are mastered. (reducer)
- **AC 3.17.4** answer is accepted only for the current item, once per round. Continue/Enter does nothing until the current item has been answered. next-round works only from the recap. The final mastering answer shows feedback, and Continue goes to the finish screen (no final recap). (reducer and page test with the Enter hotkey)
- **AC 3.17.5** Options are seeded by runId and attempt number: stable on reload, re-seeded on each re-ask (2-option items may repeat their order). Quiz and FixedSetQuiz option order is unchanged (golden test). (unit)
- **AC 3.17.6** The "Mastered N of M" role=progressbar (aria-valuenow N, aria-valuemax M) updates on each mastering answer. Feedback is announced through aria-live=polite with the remaining correct answers needed. The recap shows "Round R complete", the round's correct count, and a per-item text status. The finish screen shows "You've mastered all M items" (singular for 1), "First try: C / M" with a percentage, **Learn again** (new runId) and the browse link. (page test)
- **AC 3.17.7** Progress is kept in localStorage quizymode.learn.v1 (version 1): at most 3 runs, LRU, 7-day TTL, 256 KB cap (evict the oldest run before a write fails). Restore needs the same schema, identity, normalized scope, and valid progress (keys within itemIds, well-formed attempts, mastered/streak consistent with attempts, finished only when all items are mastered, completionEmitted only with completedAt). A collection run restores if every stored item is still in the fetched playable roster. Otherwise, or on corrupt data, it starts fresh with the amber notice. Expired and other-identity runs start silently. On a storage failure, play continues in memory and the status line is shown. (storage unit test and page test)
- **AC 3.17.8** [Privacy] Question/answer text is snapshotted only for guest category/global runs. Signed-in runs store IDs and progress (attempts keep the selected option index, never answer text) and refetch items on restore; any inaccessible item restarts the run with the notice. Entries of other identities are purged on sign-out (before the Cognito/Google redirect) and on settled app load. This amends AC 3.6.12 for Learn. (unit test, including the Google logout path)
- **AC 3.17.9** [Authenticated] A run started and finished under the same identity sends exactly one POST /api/study-sessions: runId, completedAt, total = M, correct = first-try correct, missedItemIds = first-try misses, and the source as in AC 3.6.14. Misses enter the review queue (AC 3.14.1). Restore, remount and retry never create a second session (idempotent by runId). A failed save shows "We couldn't save this session." with **Try again**, which uses the same runId. Leaving early saves and queues nothing. Learn does not dispatch quizymode:quiz-completed. (page test asserting a single call and its payload; backend test of the enqueue for a 50-item payload)
- **AC 3.17.10** [Anonymous] A finished guest run is upserted into quizymode.guest.v1 per AC 3.6.13. Both the guest entry and the study-session payload (AC 3.6.14) carry `mode: "learn"` for a Learn run, and Quiz runs carry `quiz`. Continue (3.0.21), "Your last quiz" and Practice again (3.0.15, 3.0.16) open Learn (`/learn/...`) for Learn runs and Quiz otherwise; attempted/inferred For-you categories (3.0.22) count both modes. (tests: guest storage v1 read as quiz, Learn page sends mode learn, home surfaces link to /learn, backend persists and validates mode)
- **AC 3.17.11** Analytics: quick_quiz_started, question_answered (first attempt per item only, position = roster index + 1) and quiz_completed (score = first-try correct, total = M) use context type learn, with id collection:GUID or category:NAME when it matches the pattern, and none for global scope. The API accepts learn (amend 5.4.2). The learning report shows these runs as mode learn and keeps them out of quiz drop-off and abandonment. Popular categories (3.15.2) still count Quiz category runs only. (backend tests: TrackAnalyticsEvents accepts learn; GetLearningReport separates the modes; ComputePopularCategories ignores learn)
- **AC 3.17.12** Mode switchers show **Learn** after Quiz on category (Sets, List, Flashcards, Quiz, Learn), leaf and collection scopes (amend 3.6.3 and 3.6.3a). The intro copy distinguishes Quiz (one pass), Learn (repeat until mastered) and Review (spaced, due items). At 360/375 px the strip scrolls, the page is not wider than the viewport, the active tab is scrolled into view, and an edge cue shows when tabs overflow. (categoryScopeMode test; Playwright at 375 px)
- **AC 3.17.13** #252 hotkeys work in Learn: 1-4 / A-D answer, Enter continues (and respects the guard). They are off when shortcuts are disabled, and buttons expose aria-keyshortcuts. Focus moves to the question heading, the recap h2 or the finish h2 on each view change. (page test)

### AC 3.18 Test option on Quiz: exam simulation (#255)

Test is a mode of Quiz, not a separate page. It is an honour-system simulation: the answers are present in the API payload and in `sessionStorage`; the requirement is only that the UI never renders them before submit.

- **AC 3.18.1** [Anyone] **Given** I am on Quiz for a collection, category or global scope, **when** I choose **Test** in the *Quiz mode* radiogroup or open a URL with `?mode=test`, **then** Test opens.
  - **Practice** removes `mode` from the URL.
  - `mode=test` is kept across Next, Prev, palette jumps, the initial item-URL sync and quiz-size changes. It is never carried onto Explore, List or Sets URLs.
  - Switching mode with at least one answer opens a confirmation (**Keep current run** / **Switch and restart**); confirming starts a new run ID. With no answers, the switch happens silently.
  - Daily and Review have no Test option.
- **AC 3.18.2** [Anyone] **Given** Test is selected, **then** a setup card shows the question count, the **Time limit** choices (No limit by default / 1 min per question / 2 min per question / Custom 1-600 minutes) and **Start test**.
  - The per-question presets are capped at 600 minutes.
  - A custom value outside 1-600 shows "Enter 1 to 600 minutes." and blocks Start.
  - No question, palette or timer appears before **Start test**, and no `quick_quiz_started` is sent.
  - The roster uses the #250 playable multiple-choice filter (**AC 3.6.17**), capped at the first 500 roster items ("Your test will use 500 of N"). An empty roster shows "No multiple-choice questions are available for Test here." with no run.
- **AC 3.18.3** [Anyone] **Given** a started, unsubmitted Test, **then** none of the following appear:
  - correctness styling or the correct-answer panel
  - the explanation or the "Score:" line
  - the item-details link, ratings, comments or collection controls
  - option pronunciation hints or "Read answer aloud"
  - the List/Explore/Sets switch (it is hidden while the Test runs)

  The selected option is marked with `aria-pressed="true"` and neutral styling. Choosing a different option replaces my answer, and the last answer before submit is scored. **Quiz size** is disabled.
- **AC 3.18.4** [Anyone] **Given** a started Test, **then:**
  - **Flag for review** (`aria-pressed`, and the F key when single-key shortcuts are on) toggles a flag on the current question.
  - The palette lists every question as a button named like "Question 7, answered, flagged, current", with icon and text cues as well as colour.
  - It shows answered, unanswered and flagged counts.
  - Activating an entry opens that question. On narrow screens the palette opens as a modal sheet; Escape closes it and focus returns to its toggle.
- **AC 3.18.5** [Anyone] **Given** a started Test, **when** I choose **Submit test**, **then** a modal dialog opens. It shows "N unanswered and M flagged" (or "All N questions are answered."), with focus on **Return to test**.
  - **Return to test** and Escape close it without changing anything.
  - **Show unanswered** / **Show flagged** jump to the first such question.
  - **Submit test** scores unanswered questions as wrong and moves focus to the **Test complete** heading.
  - No keyboard shortcut (including Enter or Right arrow) opens or confirms Submit. In Test, Enter and Right arrow only ever mean Next.
- **AC 3.18.6** [Anyone] **Given** a time limit, **then** a `role="timer"` region (not live) shows the time left, calculated from the stored start time and the wall clock. It changes style at 5:00 and at 1:00, and a polite status message is announced once at each.
  - At zero, including when the tab was hidden or the page is reloaded after the deadline, the Test auto-submits with completion time equal to the deadline.
  - Results then show "Time ran out. Your test was submitted automatically."
  - Answers dispatched at or after the deadline are ignored.
- **AC 3.18.7** [Anyone] **Given** I reload or come back through item URLs during a Test, **when** the schema version 2 snapshot matches the scope, roster, identity and mode, **then** answers, flags, the current state and the time left are restored.
  - The following start a new run, with the notice "We could not restore your earlier test, so we started a new one.":
    - version 1 snapshots
    - a mode mismatch
    - flags that are not unique or not in the roster
    - a limit outside 60-36000 s
    - unparseable or out-of-order timestamps
    - a non-active view without a submit time
    - a Practice run that carries Test fields
  - A started, expired run is restored and then auto-submitted, not rejected.
- **AC 3.18.8** [Anyone] **Given** a submitted Test, **then** results show:
  - correct/total and the percentage
  - the missed count, which includes unanswered questions
  - the "not answered" count
  - "Time taken m:ss of m:ss" (or just "Time taken m:ss" with no limit)
  - **Review all**, **Review mistakes** (only when something was missed), **Play again** and Browse

  **Review all** lists every question in roster order with my answer, the correct answer, the explanation, a Correct / Incorrect / Not answered text badge and a "Flagged" marker. **Review mistakes** includes unanswered questions marked "Not answered". **Back to results** returns.
- **AC 3.18.9** [Anyone] **Given** a Test is submitted (manually or by timeout), **then** exactly one completion snapshot is published. Restore, review and remount do not repeat it.
  - It is recorded through the guest history (AC 3.6.13) or the study session (AC 3.6.14) and the review queue (AC 3.14.1), unchanged.
  - Missed item IDs are wrong answers first, then unanswered questions in roster order, with at most 200 sent.
  - There are no API, schema or OpenAPI changes.
- **AC 3.18.10** [Anyone] **Given** Test results, **when** I choose **Play again**, **then** the same roster, mode and time limit return to the setup card under a new run ID, with answers, flags and the clock cleared.
- **AC 3.18.11** [Anyone] **Given** a Test, **then** analytics work as follows:
  - `quick_quiz_started` is sent on **Start test**.
  - At submit, one `question_answered` is sent per answered question, with the final correctness and the roster position; unanswered questions send nothing.
  - `quiz_completed` is then sent with score and total.
  - Nothing is sent per answer change.
  - (This amends AC 5.4.9, 5.5.4 and 5.5.2.)

Verification: `quizRun`/`testRun` reducer, restore and selector unit tests; `QuizModePage.testmode.test.tsx` page tests (setup, deferred feedback, palette, Submit dialog, fake-timer countdown and auto-submit, reload after the deadline, restore rejection, mode switching, analytics order, Play again); 375 px Playwright spec `quiz-test-mode.spec.ts`.

### AC 3.19 Share result and celebration (#253)

- **AC 3.19.1** [Anyone] **Given** I complete a quiz with at least one question (collection, category, global, daily or review), **then** a tier line appears under the unchanged "Quiz complete" heading: 100% "Perfect score!", 80 to 99% "Great job!", 50 to 79% "Good effort. Keep going!", under 50% "Keep practicing. Every miss is a lesson." The tier comes from the rounded `percentage` in `summarizeQuizRun`. **Given** total is 0, **then** there is no tier line and no Share button.
- **AC 3.19.2** [Anyone] **Given** results for a collection whose `isPublic === true`, **when** I click **Share result**, **then** the preview, copied text and native share text are `I scored {n}/{t} on "{name}" on Quizymode. Can you beat me?` followed by `{origin}/quiz/collections/{id}/{slug}?utm_source=share&utm_medium=share&utm_campaign=quiz_result&utm_content=collection`. The URL is built with `buildCollectionStudyPath("quiz", id, name)`, and **Show QR code** is available.
- **AC 3.19.3** [Owner] **Given** results for a collection whose `isPublic` is false, missing, or still loading, **then** the shared text is exactly `I scored {n}/{t} on a Quizymode quiz.`. There is no URL and no QR button, and the dialog shows "Private collection: only your score is shared, with no link." The collection name, id and slug appear nowhere in the textarea, the clipboard payload, the `navigator.share` argument, the dialog title, or any alt text.
- **AC 3.19.4** [Anyone] **Given** category results, **then** the text is `I scored {n}/{t} on a {Category name} quiz on Quizymode. Try this quiz!` plus `{origin}/quiz/{categorySlug}?...&utm_content=category`, with no keywords, search or filter params. **Given** unscoped results, **then** the text is `I scored {n}/{t} on a Quizymode quiz. Try this quiz!` plus `{origin}/quiz?...&utm_content=global`. A visit through any of these links is captured per **AC 5.7.10** and classified as `campaign` per **AC 5.7.4**.
- **AC 3.19.5** [Anyone] **Given** the Share result dialog is open, **then**:
  - **Copy** writes the full text and shows "Copied.". It resets after 3 s or when the dialog closes.
  - A copy failure, or a missing clipboard, shows "Couldn't copy. Select the text above and copy it." and focuses and selects the read-only textarea.
  - **Share via...** appears only when `navigator.share` exists. AbortError is ignored, and any other error falls back to Copy.
  - **Show QR code** appears only for linked scopes, and a QR failure shows "The QR code couldn't be created."
  - Escape, a backdrop click or the close button dismisses the dialog, and focus returns to **Share result**.
  - The dialog is a modal dialog (`role=dialog`, `aria-modal`, labelled), so study shortcuts are inert while it is open.
- **AC 3.19.6** [Anyone] **Given** a 100% score and `prefers-reduced-motion` does not match, **then** one confetti burst fires once per run id from a lazily loaded `canvas-confetti` chunk that is not part of the entry bundle. The canvas is `pointer-events: none`, is never focused, and any load failure is silent. **Given** reduced motion, `matchMedia` unavailable, or a score under 100%, **then** the module is never imported.
- **AC 3.19.7** [Anyone] **Given** I answer a question, **then** the correct option pulses and a wrong selected option shakes using only `motion-safe:` utilities, with a check or X icon (aria-hidden) alongside the existing colours. **Given** reduced motion, **then** nothing animates, and the colour, icon and text feedback are unchanged.
- **AC 3.19.8** [Anyone] **Given** the share logic lives in `useShareActions`, **then** the collection Share dialog still behaves exactly as **AC 1.7.5** describes (same labels, status strings, QR alt text and private-link warning).
