## 6. Study guides and import

### AC 6.1 Study guide CRUD and per-user size limit

**API**

- **AC 6.1.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I am authenticated and have no study guide yet, **when** I call `GET /study-guides/current`, **then** the API returns 404 Not Found (no guide exists); the body may be empty or ProblemDetails, and no study guide is created implicitly.
- **AC 6.1.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I am authenticated, **when** I call `PUT /study-guides/current` with a JSON body `{ "title": "...", "contentText": "..." }` whose UTF-8 byte length is at most my **per-user study guide byte limit**, **then** the API upserts my single private study guide (creating it if missing, replacing it if it exists) and returns 200 with `id`, `title`, `sizeBytes`, `updatedUtc`, and `expiresAtUtc`; `sizeBytes` equals the UTF-8 byte length of `contentText`, and `expiresAtUtc` is set to 14 days from the time of the save.
- **AC 6.1.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I try to save a study guide whose `contentText` exceeds my **per-user study guide byte limit**, **when** I call `PUT /study-guides/current`, **then** the API returns 400 Bad Request with a validation error explaining that the limit was exceeded (including the maximum bytes and current size); the previous guide (if any) is left unchanged.
- **AC 6.1.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I have a study guide, **when** I call `DELETE /study-guides/current`, **then** the API deletes my study guide (idempotent) and returns 204 No Content; subsequent `GET /study-guides/current` returns 404 until I create a new one.
- **AC 6.1.5** [Anonymous] **Given** I am not authenticated, **when** I call any of `GET/PUT/DELETE /study-guides/current`, **then** the API returns 401 Unauthorized.
- **AC 6.1.8** [Authenticated] **Given** I have a study guide, **when** I call `GET /study-guides/current`, **then** the response includes `expiresAtUtc` (ISO-8601 UTC) indicating when the guide will be automatically deleted.
- **AC 6.1.9** [System] **Given** a study guide's `expiresAtUtc` is in the past, **when** the daily background cleanup runs, **then** the API deletes that study guide and all cascade-related import sessions, chunks, and results; the cleanup runs approximately every 24 hours and logs the number of records deleted.
- **AC 6.1.10** [System] Saving (creating or updating) a study guide resets `expiresAtUtc` to 14 days from the save time, extending the retention window.

**UI**

- **AC 6.1.6** [Authenticated] **Given** I open the Study Guide screen, **when** the page loads, **then** I see a title input, a large text area for study guide text, preparation instructions, a byte counter and remaining bytes indicator, and Save/Delete/Cancel controls; the byte counter updates as I type, and the Save button is disabled when the content exceeds my effective study-guide limit (default **51,200 bytes / 50 KB**, unless overridden by `StudyGuideMaxBytes`).
- **AC 6.1.7** [Authenticated] **Given** I have a saved study guide, **when** I visit the Study Guide screen, **then** the title and content area are pre-filled with my latest saved values, and clicking **Start import workflow** takes me to the import wizard at `/study-guide/import`.
- **AC 6.1.11** [Authenticated] **Given** I open the Study Guide screen, **when** the page loads, **then** a persistent warning banner informs me that study guides are temporary storage and are automatically deleted 14 days after the last save; if a guide already exists, the banner also displays the exact expiry date and notes that saving again will reset the 14-day clock.

### AC 6.2 Study guide import session and chunking

**API**

- **AC 6.2.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I have a study guide, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/import/sessions` with a body containing `categoryName` (required), `navigationKeywordPath` (array of zero, one, or two keyword names), optional `defaultKeywords`, and `targetItemsPerChunk` (5–50), **then** the API validates the request, ensures a study guide exists for me, creates a `StudyGuideImportSession` tied to my guide and user, and returns 201 with `sessionId`, `studyGuideId`, `studyGuideTitle`, and `studyGuideSizeBytes`.
- **AC 6.2.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I do not have a study guide saved, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/import/sessions`, **then** the API returns 404 Not Found with an error indicating that no study guide exists.
- **AC 6.2.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I own an import session, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}/generate-chunks`, **then** the API deterministically chunks my study guide text into one or more `StudyGuideChunk` records (target ~8–10 KB, hard max ~14 KB), generates a full prompt string for each chunk, stores them, updates the session status to `ChunksGenerated`, and returns 200 with a list of chunks (id, index, title, sizeBytes).
- **AC 6.2.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I own an import session, **when** I call `GET /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}`, **then** the API returns 200 with session metadata (id, studyGuideId, categoryName, navigationKeywordPath, defaultKeywords, targetItemsPerChunk, status) plus arrays of chunks (including `promptText`), per-chunk prompt results (validation status, messages, parsed JSON string when valid), and (when present) a dedup result summary.
- **AC 6.2.5** [Authenticated] **Given** I am not the owner of the session or the session does not exist, **when** I call `GET` or `POST` on any `study-guides/import/sessions/{id}*` endpoint, **then** the API returns 404 Not Found (session is not visible outside the owner).

**UI**

- **AC 6.2.6** [Authenticated] **Given** I have a study guide and navigate to `/study-guide/import`, **when** the page loads, **then** I see a stepper-style wizard with at least: (1) Session setup (category + navigation + default keywords + target items per chunk), (2) Prompts & chunks, (3) Optional dedup, (4) Final import summary.
- **AC 6.2.7** [Authenticated] **Given** I fill out the session setup form with a category (and optional navigation keywords and default keywords) and click the button to create a session, **when** the request succeeds, **then** I advance to the prompts step and the wizard uses the returned session id for subsequent operations.

### AC 6.3 Prompt copy/paste, validation, and dedup

**API**

- **AC 6.3.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I own an import session with generated chunks, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}/chunks/{chunkIndex}/result` with `{ "rawResponseText": "<AI text>" }`, **then** the API attempts to parse `rawResponseText` as JSON, requires a top-level array of objects, runs item-level validation, and — when valid — stores an **enriched** JSON array (not raw AI text) as `parsedItemsJson`; enrichment overrides `category`, `navigationKeyword1`, and `navigationKeyword2` from session values (preventing AI hallucination or tampering), silently truncates `source` to 200 chars instead of rejecting, and does **not** add or persist repo-managed identity fields such as `seedId` or `itemId`. Extra fields in the AI response (such as `isPrivate`, `factualRisk`, or `reviewComments`) are silently ignored. The endpoint returns 200 with `validationStatus` (`Valid` or `Invalid`), validation messages, and the enriched JSON string when valid.
- **AC 6.3.1a** [System] The AI prompts for both study guide import and the standalone bulk-create flow include explicit field length limits in the schema hint: `question` ≤1000 chars, `correctAnswer` and each `incorrectAnswers` item ≤500 chars, `source` ≤200 chars (URL only), `explanation` ≤4000 chars.
- **AC 6.3.2** [Authenticated] **Given** my chunk response JSON is malformed or fails validation, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}/chunks/{chunkIndex}/result`, **then** the API returns 200 with `validationStatus = "Invalid"`, a non-empty list of human-readable messages, and `parsedItemsJson = null`; the bad response is still stored for audit.
- **AC 6.3.3** [Authenticated] **Given** my session has more than one chunk and at least one validated prompt result, **when** I call `GET /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}`, **then** the response includes a `dedupResult` object with a generated `dedupPromptText` that summarizes all validated questions and instructions for dedup; if no validated results exist yet, `dedupPromptText` may be null.
- **AC 6.3.4** [Authenticated] **Given** I own an import session, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}/dedup-result` with `{ "rawDedupResponseText": "<AI text>" }`, **then** the API validates the JSON array using the same item rules, stores the raw and parsed dedup JSON, and returns `validationStatus` and messages similar to per-chunk validation.

**UI**

- **AC 6.3.5** [Authenticated] **Given** I am on the Prompts step of the import wizard, **when** chunks have been generated, **then** I see one panel per chunk with the chunk title, size, a **Copy prompt** button (copies the full prompt), a textarea to paste the AI JSON response, and a **Validate JSON** button that calls the per-chunk result endpoint and shows a status pill (Validated / Invalid / Not validated) plus any validation messages.
- **AC 6.3.6** [Authenticated] **Given** my session has multiple chunks and at least one validated prompt result, **when** I go to the Dedup step, **then** I see the generated dedup prompt, a **Copy dedup prompt** button, a textarea to paste the deduplicated JSON array, and a **Validate dedup JSON** button; validation results are reflected in the status text and messages.

### AC 6.4 Finalize import

**API**

- **AC 6.4.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I own an import session with either (a) a valid dedup result or (b) one or more valid per-chunk results and no dedup result, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}/finalize`, **then** the API maps the validated JSON items into `AddItemsBulk.Request` payload(s) with `IsPrivate = true`, `Category` = session category, and keywords including the navigation path and any extra keywords, invokes the existing bulk handler, and returns 200 with created/duplicate/failed counts, created item ids, and any error messages.
- **AC 6.4.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /study-guides/import/sessions/{id}/finalize` when there are no valid results to import (no valid dedup and no valid per-chunk responses), **when** the request is processed, **then** the API returns 400 Bad Request with a validation error (e.g. `Import.NoItems`) explaining that there are no items to import.

**UI**

- **AC 6.4.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I am on the import wizard and at least one valid result is available, **when** I click **Finalize import**, **then** the wizard calls the finalize endpoint, disables the button while pending, and after a successful response shows a confirmation panel summarizing the import (at minimum: that items were imported as private under the selected category) plus a link or button to navigate back to Categories.

### AC 6.5 AI study guide upload (one-shot generation, #29)

Paste up to 10 KB of text and get up to 20 AI-generated multiple-choice questions from one LLM call (DeepSeek chat completions API, OpenAI-compatible, model from `DeepSeek:Model`, default `deepseek-flash`). Jobs run in an in-API background worker; job state lives in the `StudyGuideUploads` table. This one-shot flow stays DeepSeek-only: it does not use the admin vendor routes, models, or quotas of AC 5.12 / AC 6.6 (the Generate Quiz page now creates AC 6.6 documents; these endpoints remain for existing uploads and API clients).

**API**

- **AC 6.5.1** [Authenticated] **Given** no DeepSeek API key is configured (`DeepSeek:ApiKey` or env `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`), **when** I call `GET /study-guide-uploads/config`, **then** the API returns 200 with `enabled = false`, `maxTextBytes = 10240`, and `maxQuestions`; **and when** I call `POST /study-guide-uploads`, **then** the API returns 503 with problem title `StudyGuideUpload.Disabled` and does not store anything. The key is never logged or returned.
- **AC 6.5.2** [Authenticated] **Given** the feature is enabled, **when** I call `POST /study-guide-uploads` with `text` (required, at most 10,240 UTF-8 bytes), `category`, `navigationKeyword1`, `navigationKeyword2` (a valid taxonomy path), and optional `keywords` (at most 10, letters/numbers/hyphens, max 30 chars), **then** the API validates the request (400 on failure), stores a `Pending` upload owned by me with the SHA-256 hash of the normalized text (NFC, whitespace collapsed, trimmed, lowercased), enqueues it, and returns 202 with `id`, `status = "Pending"`, `isDuplicate = false`. Anonymous callers get 401.
- **AC 6.5.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I already uploaded text with the same normalized hash, **when** I call `POST /study-guide-uploads` again, **then** the API returns 200 with the existing upload `id` and `isDuplicate = true` instead of creating a new job; if the existing upload had `Failed`, it is reset to `Pending` with the newly chosen topic/keywords and re-enqueued (retry). Duplicates are per user; another user uploading the same text gets their own upload.
- **AC 6.5.4** [Authenticated] **Given** my text or keywords contain blocked language (shared profanity list), **when** I call `POST /study-guide-uploads`, **then** the API returns 400 `StudyGuideUpload.ProfanityBlocked` and stores nothing.
- **AC 6.5.5** [System] **Given** a `Pending` upload, **when** the background worker processes it, **then** it makes one DeepSeek `POST /chat/completions` call (Bearer auth, JSON mode `response_format: json_object`, low temperature, capped `max_tokens`) with a system prompt that demands strict JSON in the `{"questions":[...]}` shape, reads `choices[0].message.content`, and marks the upload `Completed` only if the reply is valid JSON with 1–20 questions, every question passes the same per-item rules as the study guide import (question, correct answer, 1–4 incorrect answers, length limits), there are no duplicate questions, and no generated text contains blocked language; otherwise it marks the upload `Failed` with a user-safe `error` (provider errors, timeouts, content-filter stops, and over-long replies are reported generically). Processing is idempotent (non-`Pending` uploads are skipped) and every `Pending` upload is re-enqueued when the API starts. Incorrect answers in the model reply are cleaned before validation instead of failing the upload: they are trimmed, and blanks, duplicates (case-insensitive, first kept) and values equal to the correct answer are dropped; a question left with no incorrect answer is dropped and the upload fails only if no question remains. At accept time (**AC 6.5.7**, `POST /study-guide-uploads/{id}/accept`) each stored question is cleaned the same way and questions left with none are skipped and not created (400 if none of the selected questions remain) (#276).
- **AC 6.5.6** [Authenticated] **Given** I own an upload, **when** I call `GET /study-guide-uploads/{id}`, **then** the API returns 200 with `status` (`Pending`, `Completed`, `Failed`), topic fields, keywords, the generated `questions`, `error`, timestamps, `acceptedUtc`, and `createdItemCount`; **given** the upload is not mine or does not exist, **then** it returns 404.
- **AC 6.5.7** [Authenticated] **Given** my upload is `Completed` and not yet accepted, **when** I call `POST /study-guide-uploads/{id}/accept` with optional `questionIndexes` (zero-based; omitted keeps all), **then** the API creates the selected questions as my **private** items through the standard bulk item-creation path (category, navigation path, and keywords from the upload; `UploadId` = the upload id), records `acceptedUtc` and `createdItemCount`, and returns 200 with created/duplicate/failed counts and created item ids. A not-ready upload returns 409 `StudyGuideUpload.NotReady`; a second accept returns 409 `StudyGuideUpload.AlreadyAccepted`; a foreign or missing upload returns 404.
- **AC 6.5.8** [Authenticated] **Given** I call `POST /study-guide-uploads` more than 10 times within an hour, **when** the limit is reached, **then** the API returns 429 with a "Too many study guide uploads" message.

**UI**

- **AC 6.5.9** [Authenticated] **Given** I open **Generate Quiz** (`/create/notes`, linked from the Create hub; legacy `/study-guide/upload` redirects here), **when** the feature is disabled, **then** the page shows "Feature unavailable" with a link to the manual study guide flow instead of the form; a 503 on submit switches the page to the same state.
- **AC 6.5.10** [Authenticated] **Superseded by AC 6.6.20:** the **Generate Quiz** page now uploads files or pasted text as study guide documents (`POST /study-guides/documents`); it no longer creates new one-shot uploads.
- **AC 6.5.11** [Authenticated] **Given** I open a link to an earlier one-shot upload (`/create/notes?id=<uploadId>`; the legacy `/study-guide/upload?id=` link redirects, AC 2.2.5), **when** the upload is `Pending`, **then** the page shows a progress state and polls every 2 seconds; **when** it is `Completed`, **then** it lists the questions with correct/incorrect answers and explanations, each with a checkbox (all checked by default), and **Add N selected to my items** calls accept and then shows how many items were added with a link to the topic; **when** it is `Failed`, **then** it shows the error and a **Try again** action. (**Try again** and **Upload another study guide** return to the Generate Quiz form, AC 6.6.20.)

### AC 6.6 Study guide documents (v2, #203)

Upload a PDF, Word (`.docx`), PowerPoint (`.pptx`), or text / Markdown study guide (or paste text). The API extracts the text, splits it into sections, and on request generates up to 20 source-quoted multiple-choice questions per section through a multi-vendor LLM router; the owner reviews them and saves the chosen ones as private items in a link-shared collection. The original file is never stored — only its SHA-256, the SHA-256 of the normalized text, and the extracted section text, which is kept until 30 days after the last activity. Generations are a shared, system-owned cache keyed by (section text hash, mode `GuideOnly`, prompt version), so identical text is generated once for everyone. All endpoints require sign-in and are owner-only (404 for other users' documents); JSON is camelCase.

**API** (#198, #199, #202)

- **AC 6.6.1** [Authenticated] **Given** I am signed in, **when** I call `GET /study-guides/documents/config`, **then** the API returns `enabled` (true when at least one vendor in my tier's saved route, free or premium, has an API key configured, AC 5.12.1 and 5.12.5), `maxFileBytes` (20 MB), `maxPages` (300), `maxTextBytes` (1,048,576), `acceptedExtensions` (`.pdf`, `.docx`, `.pptx`, `.txt`, `.md`), `freeTierDelayMinutes` (10), and my `quota` (`{ tier, dailyLimit, dailyUsed, monthlyLimit, monthlyUsed, remainingToday }`, AC 6.6.8). Limits come from the `StudyGuideDocuments` and `StudyGuideGeneration` configuration sections.
- **AC 6.6.2** [Authenticated] **Given** I am signed in, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/documents` as `multipart/form-data` with exactly one of `file` or `text` (at most `maxTextBytes`), plus `category`, `navigationKeyword1`, `navigationKeyword2` (a valid taxonomy path, same rules as AC 6.5.2), optional `title` (≤ 200 characters; default = the file name without extension, else the first line of the text), and optional `keywords` (repeated and/or comma-separated; at most 10, letters/numbers/hyphens, ≤ 30 characters), **then** the API extracts and sections the text, stores the document and its sections, and returns 201 with the `Document` (sections with status `New`, `isDuplicate = false`, my quota). Neither or both of `file`/`text` → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.FileOrTextRequired`; field validation failures → 400; an invalid category or path → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.InvalidCategory` / `InvalidNavigationPath`; blocked language in the title, keywords, or extracted text → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.ProfanityBlocked` (nothing stored). Anonymous → 401. A body larger than 21 MB is rejected (413); more than 20 creates per user per hour → 429 "Too many study guide uploads".
- **AC 6.6.3** [Authenticated] **Given** I upload a file, **then** its type is detected from its bytes, not its name: `%PDF` → PDF; a zip containing `word/document.xml` → DOCX, or `ppt/presentation.xml` → PPTX; otherwise valid UTF-8 text without NUL bytes → text (`fileType` = `txt` for `.txt`, `.md`, and pasted text). Anything else → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.UnsupportedType`. Files over 20 MB or zips that would expand beyond 100 MB → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.FileTooLarge`; more than 300 pages/slides → 400 `TooManyPages`; extracted text over 1 MiB → 400 `TextTooLarge`; no text → 400 `Empty`; damaged or password-protected files → 400 `Unreadable`; extraction longer than 30 s → 400 `ExtractionTimeout`. Parsing is read-only and never runs macros.
- **AC 6.6.4** [Authenticated] **Given** a PDF where more than half of the pages have fewer than 40 non-whitespace characters, **then** the API returns 400 `StudyGuideDocument.Scanned` ("This looks like a scan; only PDFs with selectable text are supported for now."). For PDFs with at least 3 pages, lines that repeat (digits ignored) on at least 60% of pages are removed as headers/footers, and bare page-number lines ("12", "Page 3 of 10") are always removed.
- **AC 6.6.5** [System] Sections are at most 10,240 UTF-8 bytes, packed toward 8,192: PDFs pack whole pages and PPTX whole slides (an oversized page is split at paragraph, then line, then word boundaries), each page starting with a marker line `[p. N]` / `[slide N]`; DOCX splits at Heading 1–3 / Title paragraphs, then paragraphs; text splits at Markdown headings, then blank lines. Each section has `index`, `label` (`p. 3`, `pp. 12–14`, `Slide 4`, `Slides 4–6`, or the heading text ≤ 120 characters, else `Section N`), `pageStart` / `pageEnd` (null for DOCX/text), `sizeBytes`, and a SHA-256 of its normalized text (normalized like AC 6.5.2).
- **AC 6.6.6** [Authenticated] **Given** I already have a document with the same normalized text, **when** I upload it again (any file type or pasted), **then** the API returns 200 with that document and `isDuplicate = true` (topic and title unchanged), resets its 30-day expiry, and restores section text that had expired. Duplicates are per user; another user gets their own document.
- **AC 6.6.7** [Authenticated] **Given** I own a document, **when** I call `GET /study-guides/documents/{id}`, **then** the API returns the `Document` (`id`, `title`, `fileType`, `pageCount`, topic, `keywords`, `collectionId`, `createdUtc`, `expiresUtc`, `isDuplicate = false`, `quota`, `sections`); each section has `preview` (first 240 characters without page markers, `""` once expired), `textExpired`, `status` from its attached generation (`New` none, `Queued` pending, `Processing`, `Generated`, `Failed` with a user-safe `error`), `notBeforeUtc` while queued, `cached` (Generated and I was not charged for it), `droppedCount`, and — only when Generated — `candidates` (`id`, `index`, `question`, `correctAnswer`, `incorrectAnswers`, `explanation`, `sourceQuote`, `page`, `accepted`). `GET /study-guides/documents` lists my documents newest first as `{ documents: [{ id, title, fileType, sectionCount, generatedSectionCount, acceptedCount, collectionId, createdUtc, lastActivityUtc, expiresUtc }] }`. Another user's or a missing document → 404.
- **AC 6.6.8** [Authenticated] **Given** my tier is Free (default) or Premium (admin setting `StudyGuidePremium = "true"`, AC 5.1.3b), **then** I may be charged at most 10 sections per UTC day and 60 per UTC calendar month (Premium: 50 / 1,000), unless `StudyGuideDailySections` / `StudyGuideMonthlySections` override the limits. Usage is the sum of my charges in the current UTC day / month; `remainingToday` = min(daily left, monthly left). Sections served from the shared cache or attached to a generation someone else already queued cost 0.
- **AC 6.6.9** [Authenticated] **Given** I own a document, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/generate` with `{ sectionIds }` (1–50 sections of this document; otherwise 400 / `StudyGuideDocument.InvalidSections`), **then** for each section: already attached to a queued, processing, or generated generation → no change, cost 0; a Completed generation exists for its text → attached, cost 0 (`cachedCount`); a Pending/Processing one exists → attached, cost 0, and if I am Premium the job becomes premium and due now; a Failed one exists → reset to Pending and charged 1; otherwise a new Pending generation is created and charged 1. New/reset jobs are due immediately for Premium and after `freeTierDelayMinutes` (10) for Free. The API returns 202 `{ queuedCount, cachedCount, chargedSections, quota }`. All-or-nothing: if the charge would exceed my daily or monthly limit, it returns 429 `StudyGuideDocument.QuotaExceeded` with a message naming the limit and queues or charges nothing. A section whose text expired and that needs a charge → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.SectionExpired`. No vendor with a key in my tier's route (premium route for Premium users, free route otherwise) → 503 `StudyGuideDocument.Disabled`, checked before anything is charged, attached, or queued (so a cached-only request from such a user is also refused). Order of checks: validation 400 → document not found 404 → 503 Disabled → 400 InvalidSections / SectionExpired → 429 → 503 BudgetReached. Jobs queued before an admin route change that leave no keyed vendor still fail and are refunded (AC 6.6.13).
- **AC 6.6.10** [Authenticated] **Given** the global budget — section generations queued (created or retried) in the current UTC month across all users — plus this request's charged sections would exceed `StudyGuideGeneration:MonthlySectionBudget` (default 1,000), **when** I call generate, **then** the API returns 503 `StudyGuideDocument.BudgetReached` and charges nothing.
- **AC 6.6.11** [System] **Given** queued generations, **then** the in-API `StudyGuideGenerationWorker` polls every 15 s and claims up to 2 due jobs (Pending with `notBeforeUtc` passed, or Processing whose 5-minute lease expired), premium first, by atomically moving them to Processing with a new lease and incrementing `attempts` (PostgreSQL `UPDATE … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, so concurrent API instances never claim the same job). A job claimed more than 3 times is Failed and refunded. The queue lives in Postgres; nothing special happens on startup. The worker does nothing when no vendor key is configured.
- **AC 6.6.12** [System] **Given** a claimed job, **then** the processor takes the route for the job's tier (AC 5.12.1), skips vendors without a key, and calls each vendor in order with the admin-chosen model through one OpenAI-compatible client (`POST {base}/chat/completions`, Bearer key, `response_format: json_object`, temperature 0.2, `max_tokens` 8000; OpenAI reasoning models such as `gpt-5*` get `max_completion_tokens` and no temperature). Base URLs: DeepSeek `https://api.deepseek.com`, OpenAI `https://api.openai.com/v1`, Gemini `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/openai`. Every vendor call is logged as an `LlmAttempts` row (vendor, model, outcome, tokens, duration, HTTP status). The HTTP status is recorded for every reply that was received (including 200 replies that are unusable) and left empty for a timeout, a network failure, or a missing key. A non-2xx reply is an `AuthError` for 401; for a 403 whose body (case-insensitive) contains "api key", "api_key", "apikey", "api-key", "authenticat" or "credential"; or for a 400 whose body mentions "API key". Any other reply, including a 403 without that wording (for example an unsupported region or model access), is a `ProviderError`. Either outcome falls through to the next vendor.
- **AC 6.6.13** [System] **Given** a vendor reply, **then** prompt v2 asks for strict JSON `{"questions":[{question, correctAnswer, incorrectAnswers (1–4), explanation, sourceQuote, page}]}`, up to 20 questions only from the supplied section text, with `sourceQuote` copied verbatim (20–300 characters) and page markers in the text. Each question is kept only if it passes the item field rules, its `sourceQuote` — normalized (NFC, lowercase, curly quotes/apostrophes and dashes unified, line-break hyphenation and whitespace collapsed, leading/trailing ellipses and quotes stripped) — is at least 15 characters and occurs in the normalized section text without page markers, it has no blocked language, and it does not repeat another question; `page` comes from the marker before the quote (else the model's page if it is in the section). Dropped questions are counted in `droppedCount`. A provider error, auth error (rejected key), timeout, content-filter stop, invalid JSON, or low quote yield (nothing kept, or fewer than half of the returned questions kept) moves on to the next vendor. On success the job is Completed with its candidates, vendor, model, and tokens, and its stored source text is cleared. If every vendor fails but one returned at least one verified question, the best such result is kept; otherwise the job is Failed with a generic user-safe error and every charge for it is refunded (deleted).
- **AC 6.6.14** [Authenticated] **Given** generated candidates on my document, **when** I call `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/accept` with `{ candidates: [{ id, question?, correctAnswer?, incorrectAnswers?, explanation? }] }` (1–200; edits validated with item rules: question ≤ 1000, answers ≤ 500, 1–4 incorrect answers, explanation ≤ 3,500), **then** the API creates the selected (edited) questions as my **private** items through the standard bulk item-creation path (category, navigation path, and keywords from the document; `source` empty; `UploadId` = the document id), appends `\n\nFrom "<title>", <section label>` to each explanation, adds the new items to the document's collection, and returns 200 `{ collectionId, createdCount, duplicateCount, failedCount, itemIds }`. The first accept creates that collection — name = document title, description "Generated from study guide <title>", owner = me, `IsPublic = false` (owner only until I share it; #296); later accepts reuse it, or create a new one if I deleted it. Edited candidates whose wrong answers repeat each other or equal the correct answer are rejected with 400 `StudyGuideDocument.InvalidEdit` and nothing is saved (#276).
- **AC 6.6.15** [Authenticated] **Given** I accept candidates, **then** candidates already saved from this document are skipped and counted in `duplicateCount` (as are questions that duplicate my existing items or each other); a candidate that is not from a generated section of this document → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.InvalidCandidates` (nothing saved); blocked language in edits → 400 `StudyGuideDocument.ProfanityBlocked`; another user's document → 404.
- **AC 6.6.16** [System] Uploading a duplicate, generating, and accepting each set the document's `lastActivityUtc` to now and `expiresUtc` to 30 days later. The daily study guide cleanup (AC 6.1.9) deletes documents past `expiresUtc` that have no saved questions (their sections cascade) and, for documents with saved questions, keeps the document but clears its section text (`textExpired = true`); shared generations and candidates, accepted items, and collections are never deleted by expiry. `LlmAttempts` rows older than 90 days are trimmed by the same cleanup.
- **AC 6.6.17** [System] LLM vendor HTTP calls (the documents flow and the AC 6.5 paste flow) do not use the default HTTP resilience pipeline from service defaults (10 s attempt timeout, 30 s total, automatic retries). Each call is limited only by the configured vendor timeout (default 120 s), and failures are handled by the app's own vendor fallback, so a long completion is not cut off and a billable request is not silently retried (#206).

**UI** (#200)

- **AC 6.6.20** [Authenticated] **Given** I open **Generate Quiz** (`/create/notes`), **when** `GET /study-guides/documents/config` returns `enabled = true`, **then** I see two keyboard-operable tabs (`role="tab"`, arrow keys switch): **Upload file** (drop zone plus a labeled file input whose `accept` list comes from `acceptedExtensions`, with the size/page limits shown and a note that scanned PDFs are not supported) and **Paste text** (textarea with a live UTF-8 byte counter against `maxTextBytes`); plus optional **Title**, the shared category / primary topic / subtopic picker, and optional comma-separated **Keywords**, and a **My study guides** link. Before calling the API the form validates with Zod: a file is chosen (extension in the accepted list, not empty, at most `maxFileBytes`) or text is non-blank and within `maxTextBytes`; topic fields are present; title ≤ 200 characters; keywords well formed (≤ 10, letters/numbers/hyphens, ≤ 30 chars). An over-limit file or text disables **Upload study guide**. Submitting posts multipart `POST /study-guides/documents` (`file` or `text`, `title`, `category`, `navigationKeyword1`, `navigationKeyword2`, repeated `keywords`) and then navigates to `/study-guides/documents/{id}`; a duplicate (`isDuplicate = true`) shows "You already uploaded this study guide, so here is your existing copy." on the document page. API errors show the ProblemDetails detail (e.g. scanned PDF, unsupported type); 413 shows the file size limit; 429 a "wait a while" message; when config `enabled = false` or the API returns 503, the page shows "Feature unavailable" with links to the manual study guide flow and to My study guides.
- **AC 6.6.21** [Authenticated] **Given** I open `/study-guides/documents/{id}` for my document, **when** it loads, **then** the header shows the title, file type, page count, "N of M sections generated", the topic path, "Text kept until <date>", and an **Open collection** link (`/explore/collections/{collectionId}`) once the document has a collection; a missing or foreign document shows "could not be found" with a link back to My study guides.
- **AC 6.6.22** [Authenticated] **Given** the document page, **when** I use the **Choose sections** picker, **then** each section row has a labeled checkbox (the section label), a preview, the size, and a status chip with text — **New**, **Ready, free** (Generated and not charged to me), **Queued**, **Generating**, **Done**, **Failed** (with the error), or **Expired** (text no longer kept) — and only New/Failed sections whose text is kept can be selected. The initial selection is the first **New** sections in document order up to `quota.remainingToday` (max 50); generated and cached sections are never preselected and never count. A live counter shows "X of Y sections today" and "X of Y this month · <tier> plan" (used + selected), plus "N sections left today". **Generate N sections** is disabled when nothing is selected, the selection exceeds the sections left today (with an inline "more sections than you have left today" alert), or exceeds 50. Generating calls `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/generate` with the selected ids, clears the selection, refreshes the document, and shows how many sections were queued / ready for free / charged; 429 shows the API's quota message, 503 `BudgetReached` says generation is paused for the month, and other 503s say AI generation is unavailable.
- **AC 6.6.23** [Authenticated] **Given** any section is **Queued** or **Processing**, **when** the document page is open, **then** it polls `GET /study-guides/documents/{id}` every 3 seconds until none are; for a Free-tier user with queued sections whose `notBeforeUtc` is in the future it shows "Free plan: generation starts within about N minutes" (N rounded up from the latest `notBeforeUtc`), otherwise "Generating questions… This page updates automatically."
- **AC 6.6.24** [Authenticated] **Given** sections are **Generated**, **when** I review them, **then** questions are grouped by section label and numbered across the document; each shows the correct and wrong answers (with screen-reader labels), the explanation, and the source quote as `From your guide, p. 14: “…”` (falls back to the section label when the candidate has no page); sections with dropped questions show "N questions dropped: not found in your guide". Unaccepted questions have an **Include question N** checkbox (checked by default) and an **Edit** action that opens inline labeled fields (question, correct answer, 1–4 wrong answers with add/remove, explanation) validated with Zod using item limits (question ≤ 1000, answers ≤ 500, explanation ≤ 3500 characters) before the edit is kept; accepted questions show a **Saved** badge and no checkbox. **Save N selected** posts `POST /study-guides/documents/{id}/accept` with `{ candidates: [{ id }] }` for unedited and `{ id, question, correctAnswer, incorrectAnswers, explanation }` for edited questions (in batches of at most 200), then shows "Saved N questions to your collection" (with duplicate / failed counts when non-zero) and an **Open collection** link to `/explore/collections/{collectionId}`. Edits whose wrong answers equal the correct answer or each other are rejected inline with "Wrong answers must be different from the correct answer." / "Wrong answers must be different from each other." (#276).
- **AC 6.6.25** [Authenticated] **Given** I open **My study guides** (`/study-guides/documents`, linked from Generate Quiz and the Create hub), **when** the list loads, **then** each document shows its title, "X of Y sections generated · Z questions saved", last activity and "Text kept until <date>", a **Collection** link when one exists, and a **Continue** button to its document page; with no documents I see an empty state linking to the upload page. Under the description, once both the list and `GET /study-guides/documents/config` have loaded, a polite live region (`aria-live="polite"`) shows two lines: "This month: X of Y sections used (free|premium plan)." (the monthly quota, AC 6.6.8; "This month: X sections used (… plan)." when the monthly limit is 0, "1 section used" for a single section) and "Across all your guides: N question(s) saved in M study guide(s)." (the sum of `acceptedCount` and the number of guides, current totals rather than monthly; correct singular forms; numbers use thousands separators). With no guides the zeros are shown above the empty state. If the config request fails the summary is omitted and the list still works; if the list fails only the existing error is shown. Tokens and cost are never shown.
- **AC 6.6.26** [Authenticated] The document pages are lazy-loaded routes behind sign-in (anonymous users are sent to sign in), the **Create** navigation item stays highlighted on `/study-guides/*`, and the page title fallback is "My Study Guides".

## AC 7. Developer tooling

### AC 7.1 Automated user guide screenshots

- **AC 7.1.0** [Dev tooling] User-guide screenshots are captured from either production (`https://www.quizymode.com`) or local (`http://localhost:7000`) using the existing Playwright screenshot projects for desktop and mobile. Production publishing does not commit generated screenshots back into the repo; GitHub Actions stores them in temporary build folders, assembles a static Pages site artifact, and deploys only the latest published guide.
- **AC 7.1.1** [Dev tooling] Running `npx playwright test --project=screenshots` authenticates as the test user (credentials from `TEST_USER_EMAIL` / `TEST_USER_PASSWORD` env vars), visits each major signed-in route, and saves a full-page PNG screenshot to `docs/user-guide/screenshots/user/{slug}.png`. Running `npx playwright test --project=screenshots-mobile` captures the same slug inventory to `docs/user-guide/screenshots/mobile/{slug}.png` using a phone-sized browser profile. The target URL is controlled by `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` (defaults to `https://www.quizymode.com`).
- **AC 7.1.2** [Dev tooling] Running `node scripts/generate-user-guide.js --guide desktop` reads desktop screenshots and rebuilds the desktop markdown guide; `--guide mobile` rebuilds the mobile markdown guide; `--all` rebuilds both. Running `node scripts/build-user-guide-site.js` assembles one static user-guide site with `/assets/desktop/` and `/assets/mobile/` screenshots, a client-side Desktop or Mobile toggle, automatic initial mode selection from viewport width, and persisted user preference in `localStorage`.
- **AC 7.1.3** [Dev tooling] `playwright/.auth/` (session tokens) and `docs/user-guide/screenshots/` (generated artifacts) are excluded from source control via `.gitignore`.

### AC 7.2 Local E2E runner scripts

- **AC 7.2.1** [Dev tooling] `scripts/run-e2e-local-smoke.ps1` (Windows) and `scripts/run-e2e-local-smoke.sh` (Mac/Linux/WSL) start the local Aspire host and React dev server, wait for both to become healthy, run Playwright auth setup, then execute only tests tagged `@smoke` via `--project=smoke`.
- **AC 7.2.2** [Dev tooling] `scripts/run-e2e-local-full.ps1` and `scripts/run-e2e-local-full.sh` perform the same startup sequence and then execute the full E2E suite via `--project=e2e-full`.
- **AC 7.2.3** [Dev tooling] Both runner pairs share logic extracted into `scripts/_e2e-common.ps1` and `scripts/_e2e-common.sh` (prefixed `_` to signal they are not runnable directly). Shared functions cover: prerequisite checks, Aspire/React process management, health-poll loops, auth setup invocation, environment teardown (including on Ctrl+C), and a post-run summary that parses `playwright-report/results.json` and prints an AC-ID table.
- **AC 7.2.4** [Dev tooling] All four runner scripts read credentials exclusively from `TEST_USER_EMAIL` and `TEST_USER_PASSWORD` environment variables; no credentials are hardcoded. The `PLAYWRIGHT_BASE_URL` environment variable is set to `http://localhost:7000` before Playwright runs so the config targets the local dev stack instead of production.
- **AC 7.2.5** [Dev tooling] E2E test files live under `playwright/e2e/`. Tests tagged `@smoke` (substring in the test title) are included in the smoke project; all tests in that directory run under `e2e-full`.
- **AC 7.2.6** [Dev tooling] Each local runner script saves Playwright test artifacts (screenshots, traces) to a timestamped subfolder under `playwright/test-results/` named `smoke-YYYY-MM-DD_HH-mm-ss` or `full-YYYY-MM-DD_HH-mm-ss`. The folder path is printed at the end of the run. Subfolders older than 7 days are deleted automatically at the end of each run. The `smoke` and `e2e-full` Playwright projects are configured with `screenshot: "on"` so a screenshot is captured after every test.
- **AC 7.2.7** [Dev tooling] Local Playwright runs fail fast by default: failed tests are **not retried**, the per-test timeout is short (about 15 seconds), and default action / expectation waits are only a few seconds so broken selectors do not consume a minute per failure.

### AC 7.3 E2E test coverage baseline

- **AC 7.3.1** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/auth.spec.ts` covers the login flow (AC 4.3): login page renders the sign-in form; valid credentials redirect away from `/login`; invalid credentials show an error without navigating away. Tests run without pre-loaded auth state so they exercise the full sign-in path.
- **AC 7.3.2** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/categories.spec.ts` covers category browsing (AC 3.1, 3.4): `/categories` shows category cards; clicking a category with items navigates to the scoped URL; the category Sets view shows mode tabs (**List**, **Quiz**) and keyword buckets.
- **AC 7.3.3** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/items.spec.ts` covers item access (AC 2.3, 3.5): the **List** tab shows item links after switching modes; clicking an item opens the detail page with Question and Answer sections; the back control returns to the same list URL.
- **AC 7.3.4** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/collections.spec.ts` covers the collections page (AC 1.1, 1.3, 1.9): page loads with **Mine / Bookmarked / Discover** tabs; authenticated users see a create-collection action; switching to the Discover tab updates the URL query string to `?tab=discover`.
- **AC 7.3.5** [Dev tooling] `playwright/e2e/quiz.spec.ts` covers quiz mode (AC 3.6): quiz loads with a question and answer options after navigating from the category Sets view; selecting an answer reveals the correct answer; the Next button advances to the next question; the score counter is visible throughout.

### AC 7.4 Autonomous development and release

- **AC 7.4.1** [Dev tooling] The scheduled PowerShell controller advances enabled Codex and Claude queues without a human PR-review or release-approval gate. A candidate becomes releasable only after exact-SHA testing, fresh independent agent review, published issue/PR evidence, and the configured required GitHub checks pass.
- **AC 7.4.2** [Dev tooling] Controller, provider, merge, deployment, rollback, and issue-finalization side effects use external durable checkpoints and resume without duplicating a PR merge or rollback dispatch. Pausing prevents new stages and releases while allowing an in-flight merge/deployment/rollback to reach a recorded outcome.
- **AC 7.4.3** [Dev tooling] A candidate containing a destructive migration cannot merge until automation creates a fresh production backup, restores it into an isolated local rehearsal database, and applies the candidate EF migrations successfully. Database rollback is not automatic; migrations must preserve compatibility with the previous application revision. A migration counts as destructive when its forward `Up()` body (or a raw SQL script as a whole) drops a table, column, schema, or database, truncates, or deletes without a `WHERE`, in SQL text or through EF Core's `DropTable`, `DropColumn`, or `DropSchema` builder calls; the `Down()` rollback body is ignored, so additive migrations are not flagged.
- **AC 7.4.4** [Dev tooling] If the merge-correlated deployment or configured production smoke check fails, automation dispatches an application rollback to the previous main SHA for both LightSail and S3/CloudFront, records the rollback outcome, keeps the issue open, and halts subsequent releases.

## AC 8. Performance characteristics

### AC 8.1 Database query behaviour

- **AC 8.1.1** `GET /collections` retrieves item counts for all user collections in a single batched `GROUP BY` query, not a correlated subquery per collection.
- **AC 8.1.2** `GET /ideas` and related idea-board endpoints aggregate rating counts and averages in SQL (a `GROUP BY` projection); rating rows are not loaded into application memory for aggregation.
- **AC 8.1.3** Multi-keyword item filtering (`?keywords=a,b`) uses a single correlated `COUNT` subquery with AND semantics, not one `EXISTS` subquery per keyword.
- **AC 8.1.4** Admin page-view analytics computes the authenticated/anonymous page-view split in a single `GROUP BY IsAuthenticated` query, not separate count queries. Session counts come from one additional query returning each session's first hit, which supplies the unique-session total and the auth split together.
- **AC 8.1.4a** Admin session analytics aggregates in SQL with two queries whose result size is bounded by **session** count, not hit count: one `GROUP BY SessionId` rollup and one boundary query returning each session's first and last hit. Summary, histogram, and landing-page figures are then derived in memory from those rows; per-hit rows are never loaded wholesale. Country and user-email lookups are performed only for the visible page of sessions.
- **AC 8.1.4b** Session analytics queries use provider-agnostic LINQ (`GroupBy`, `Count(Distinct)`, joins) rather than PostgreSQL-only constructs such as `DISTINCT ON`, `BOOL_OR`, or window functions, so they execute identically against the SQLite in-memory provider used by the test fixture.
- **AC 8.1.5** All read-only item, comment, and rating queries use `AsNoTracking()` so EF Core does not maintain change-tracking state for entities that are never modified.
- **AC 8.1.6** The database has indexes on `CollectionItems(CollectionId)`, `CollectionItems(ItemId)`, `LOWER(Keywords.Name)`, and `LOWER(Categories.Name)` to support efficient joins and case-insensitive name lookups.
- **AC 8.1.7** `POST /ideas` and `PUT /ideas/{id}` do not load all of the author's ideas into memory: the 24-hour create quota is a SQL `COUNT` over the author's ideas created in the window, and the exact-duplicate check (AC 4.9.4/4.9.5) first narrows in SQL to the author's ideas whose lower-cased title matches, then compares the normalized title, problem, and proposed change of only those candidates.
- **AC 8.1.8** `Items` and `Collections` have a stored, generated `SearchVector` (`tsvector`, English configuration) with a GIN index each (`IX_Items_SearchVector`, `IX_Collections_SearchVector`), and `Collections` has a trigram GIN index on `lower("Name")` (`IX_Collections_Name_Trgm`, extension `pg_trgm`). Items weigh Question (A), CorrectAnswer (B), Explanation (C); collections weigh Name (A), Description (B). The index is not partial, because the caller's own private items are searched too.
- **AC 8.1.9** The `SearchVector` columns are created with raw SQL in migration `AddGlobalSearchIndexes` and are deliberately **not** part of the EF model (so entity loads never select them and `EnsureCreated` still works on SQLite in tests); `dotnet ef migrations has-pending-model-changes` reports none. A future change to the type or length of `Question`, `CorrectAnswer`, `Explanation`, `Name` or `Description` must drop and recreate the generated column first (documented in `Migrations/README.md`). Keyword names are not full-text indexed: topic search runs in memory over the YAML taxonomy.

### AC 8.2 Route-level code splitting (PERF-001)

- **AC 8.2.1** [Anonymous] **Given** a visitor opens `/`, **then** the browser downloads only the eager bundle (home, categories, items list, explore/flashcards, quiz, daily quiz, collections, featured, item detail and comments, login and signup) plus shared vendor chunks; no admin (`/admin/*`), authoring (`/create`, `/create/question`, `/create/import`, `/items/bulk-create`, `/items/:id/edit`), study guide (`/study-guide`, `/study-guide/import`, `/create/notes`, `/study-guides/documents`, `/study-guides/documents/:id`), review queue (`/review`), community (`/ideas`, `/roadmap`, `/feedback`) or legal/about (`/about`, `/privacy*`, `/terms*`) page code is fetched; `/search` is lazy as well.
- **AC 8.2.2** [Any user] **Given** the user navigates to a lazy route, **then** the existing loading spinner renders inside the layout (header and navigation stay visible) until the page chunk arrives, then the page renders.
- **AC 8.2.3** [Any user] **Given** a lazy page chunk fails to load (for example a stale tab after a deploy), **then** the app reloads the page automatically at most once per tab session (guarded in `sessionStorage`); if loading fails again, or storage is unavailable, it shows "This page failed to load" with a **Retry** button that reloads the page only when clicked (browsers cache a failed module import for the life of the page), so it never loops. A successful lazy load clears the guard. Navigating to another route clears the failure view. Non-chunk render errors still reach the app-level error boundary and are reported per AC 5.6.
- **AC 8.2.4** [System] The deploy S3 sync keeps earlier hashed `*.js` and `*.css` files (they are excluded from `--delete`), so tabs running an older `index.html` can still open lazy routes after a deploy.

## AC 9. Text-to-speech (browser TTS)

### AC 9.1 Quiz mode playback

- **AC 9.1.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] A speaker-icon button appears next to the "Question" heading in Quiz mode. Clicking it speaks the question text via the browser Web Speech API.
- **AC 9.1.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When the answer is revealed in Practice-mode Quiz (for Test see AC 9.1.5), a speaker-icon button appears next to the "Correct Answer" label. Clicking it speaks the answer text.
- **AC 9.1.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Starting a new TTS utterance cancels any utterance already in progress so speech does not overlap.
- **AC 9.1.4** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Clicking the same speaker button again while its text is still playing stops that utterance instead of restarting it.
- **AC 9.1.5** [Anyone] **Given** a started, unsubmitted Test, **then** **Read question aloud** stays available, but "Read answer aloud" and option pronunciation hints are not rendered. Speech in progress is cancelled when the Test is submitted or times out.

### AC 9.2 Explore (flashcard) mode playback

- **AC 9.2.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] A speaker-icon button appears in the question-face header of the flashcard in Explore mode. Clicking it speaks the question text without flipping the card.
- **AC 9.2.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] A speaker-icon button appears in the answer-face header of the flashcard in Explore mode. Clicking it speaks the answer text without flipping the card back.
- **AC 9.2.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Clicking the same speaker button again while its text is still playing stops that utterance instead of restarting it.

### AC 9.3 Lifecycle and fallback

- **AC 9.3.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When the user navigates away from a Quiz or Explore page, any in-progress speech is cancelled immediately.
- **AC 9.3.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] If `window.speechSynthesis` or `SpeechSynthesisUtterance` is unavailable, the speaker buttons are not rendered and the application does not throw.
- **AC 9.3.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] No backend calls, database fields, or cloud services are involved in TTS playback; all processing is client-side only.

### AC 9.4 List mode "Listen All"

- **AC 9.4.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] In list mode (category items view and collection detail view), a "Listen All" button appears in the control bar when `window.speechSynthesis` is available.
- **AC 9.4.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Pressing "Listen All" begins sequential TTS playback: speaks the question, pauses 2 seconds, then speaks the correct answer (regardless of the "Show/Hide answers" toggle), then moves to the next item, repeating until all items in the current list are read.
- **AC 9.4.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] While playback is active, the button label changes to "Pause". Pressing it cancels in-progress speech and pauses playback; the button changes to "Resume".
- **AC 9.4.4** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Pressing "Resume" restarts playback from the beginning of the item that was paused on.
- **AC 9.4.5** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When the last item's answer has been spoken, playback stops automatically and the button returns to "Listen All".
- **AC 9.4.6** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Navigating away from the list view cancels any in-progress "Listen All" playback.

## AC 10. Featured page

A public page at `/featured` shows Admin-curated Sets and Collections with modification timestamps and sorting.

### AC 10.1 Data model

- **AC 10.1.1** [Admin] A `FeaturedItems` table stores entries of two types: `Set` (category slug + optional L1/L2 navigation keyword names) and `Collection` (reference to an existing Collection by ID). Each entry has a `DisplayName`, `SortOrder`, `CreatedBy`, and `CreatedAt`.
- **AC 10.1.2** [Admin] An `Item` record gains an `UpdatedAt` (`DateTime?`) column. It is set to `DateTime.UtcNow` whenever an item is modified via `UpdateItem`, `UpdateItemCategories`, `SetItemVisibility`, `ApproveItem`, or `RejectItem`.

### AC 10.2 API — Public

- **AC 10.2.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] `GET /featured` returns two arrays: `sets` and `collections`, each ordered by `SortOrder ASC, DisplayName ASC`.
- **AC 10.2.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Each Set entry includes: `id`, `displayName`, `categorySlug`, `navKeyword1`, `navKeyword2`, `itemCount` (count of public items matching the set's category/keyword path), `lastModifiedAt` (max of `COALESCE(UpdatedAt, CreatedAt)` across those items, or `null` if no items exist), `sortOrder`. The keyword name-to-ID lookup used to filter items **must only consider public (`IsPrivate = false`) keywords and must deduplicate by name** (the `Keywords` unique index is on `(Name, CreatedBy, IsPrivate)`, so the same name can appear multiple times across different creators; the lookup takes the first match per lowercase name to avoid a duplicate-key crash).
- **AC 10.2.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Each Collection entry includes: `id`, `collectionId`, `displayName`, `description`, `itemCount`, `lastModifiedAt` (max of `COALESCE(UpdatedAt, CreatedAt)` across items in the collection, or `null` if no items), `sortOrder`.

### AC 10.3 API — Admin

- **AC 10.3.1** [Admin] `GET /admin/featured` returns all featured items with full fields (type, display name, category/keyword path or collection reference, sort order, created at).
- **AC 10.3.2** [Admin] `POST /admin/featured` adds a new featured item. Requires `type` ("Set" or "Collection"), `displayName` (max 200 chars), and type-specific fields (`categorySlug` + `navKeyword1` for sets; `collectionId` for collections). `sortOrder` is optional; if omitted, the item is appended after the highest-ordered item of its type. Returns 201 with `{ id }`. Returns 409 Conflict if the identical set (`categorySlug` + `navKeyword1` + `navKeyword2`) or collection (`collectionId`) is already featured.
- **AC 10.3.3** [Admin] `PATCH /admin/featured/{id}` updates `displayName` and/or `sortOrder` on an existing item. Returns 204. Returns 404 if not found.
- **AC 10.3.4** [Admin] `DELETE /admin/featured/{id}` removes a featured item. Returns 204. Returns 404 if not found.
- **AC 10.3.5** [Anonymous, Authenticated (non-admin)] All `/admin/featured` endpoints return 403 Forbidden.

### AC 10.4 UI — Featured page

- **AC 10.4.1** [Anonymous, Authenticated] A "Featured" link appears in the main nav between Categories and Collections (desktop and mobile).
- **AC 10.4.2** [Anonymous, Authenticated] `/featured` renders a full-bleed dark page with a tab switcher (Sets | Collections) and sort controls (Last Modified | Name).
- **AC 10.4.3** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Each Set card shows: display name, category › L1 › L2 path as subtitle, item count, and last modified date. Clicking navigates to `/categories/:categorySlug/:kw1/:kw2?from=featured`.
- **AC 10.4.4** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Each Collection card shows: display name, description (if any), item count, and last modified date. Clicking navigates to the collection detail page with `?from=featured`.
- **AC 10.4.5** [Anonymous, Authenticated] Sorting by "Last Modified" orders cards newest-first. Sorting by "Name" orders alphabetically. Default is "Last Modified".
- **AC 10.4.6** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When navigated from Featured (i.e. `?from=featured` is present), both CategoriesPage and CollectionDetailPage display a "← Back to Featured" link at the top of the page content.
- **AC 10.4.7** [Anonymous, Authenticated] When a tab (Sets or Collections) has no items, the page shows an empty state: "No featured {tab} yet. Check back soon."

### AC 10.5 UI — Admin Featured page

- **AC 10.5.1** [Admin] `/admin/featured` is linked from the Admin Dashboard.
- **AC 10.5.2** [Admin] The page has a tab switcher (Sets | Collections). Each tab shows two panels: an "Add" panel on the left and the current ordered list on the right.
- **AC 10.5.3** [Admin] The Add panel has a free-text search box. For Sets, it filters all `cat › L1` and `cat › L1 › L2` taxonomy combinations client-side (loaded from `GET /taxonomy`). For Collections, it searches public collections via `GET /collections/discover`. Each result row has a checkbox. Already-featured items are greyed out and their checkboxes are disabled.
- **AC 10.5.4** [Admin] Checking one or more rows and clicking "Add N selected" bulk-adds them via parallel `POST /admin/featured` calls. Display names are auto-generated from the taxonomy path (e.g. `Cloud Computing › Aws › Saa C03`) or collection name. Items are appended at the bottom of the ordered list (sortOrder auto-assigned by backend).
- **AC 10.5.5** [Admin] The current list shows items ordered by `sortOrder`. Each row has ↑/↓ buttons to reorder: clicking swaps the item's position with its neighbor and updates `sortOrder` on all affected items via `PATCH`. The first item has no ↑ button; the last has no ↓ button.
- **AC 10.5.6** [Admin] Each row has a pencil icon for inline rename (display name only) and a trash icon to remove. Changes take effect immediately after save.
