## AC 11. Global search (SEARCH-001..003, #324)

**Design:** One endpoint, `GET /search`, searches topics (the YAML taxonomy, in memory), discoverable public collections, public questions, and the signed-in caller's own content. Topics match on slugs and descriptions plus a hand-kept alias list (for example `ai` also reads as "artificial intelligence", so "artificial intelligence" finds `tech › ai-ml`), with prefix and one-typo tolerance. Collections use PostgreSQL full-text search plus trigram typo tolerance on the name; questions use full-text search only. Results come back grouped, each group shaped `{ results, hasMore }`. **The search text is never stored or logged**: it is not written to page views (AC 5.3.21), analytics events (AC 5.4.11), or request logs (AC 11.14). Topic counts are **public items only**, refreshed lazily at most every 10 minutes.

**Deviations from the issue text (also listed in the PR):** (1) there are no keyword full-text or trigram indexes, topic search is in memory; (2) the AI topic is `tech › ai-ml` (there is no `artificial-intelligence` node); (3) categories and topics are shown by slug with their YAML description, because the taxonomy has no separate display names; (4) groups are `{ results, hasMore }` objects, not bare arrays; (5) recent searches are not stored anywhere in v1, so the Privacy Policy is unchanged; (6) the legacy `StudyGuide` is not searched (it is being retired by #296); only study guide documents are; (7) the header shows the inline box at `xl` and above and the full-screen overlay below `xl` (there is no popover at 1024-1279 px), with no "recent searches" and no offline state; the 429 notice is static with a manual retry.

**API**

- **AC 11.1** [Anonymous] **Given** I call `GET /search?q=artificial intelligence`, **then** the API returns **200** and `groups.subcategories.results[0]` is `{ categorySlug: "tech", l1: "ai-ml", l2: null, ... }`. A typo such as `artifical` returns the same top result. Anyone may call the endpoint; no sign-in is needed.
- **AC 11.2** [Anonymous] **Given** `q`, **then** it is trimmed and must be 2-100 characters, otherwise **400**. `types` is a comma-separated list of `categories`, `subcategories`, `collections`, `items`, `mine` (case-insensitive; default: all); an unknown value returns **400**. `limit` is 1-20 (default 5) and `offset` is 0-200 (default 0), otherwise **400**. Each group returns at most `limit` results and `hasMore` is true only while `offset + limit` is at most 200 and more rows exist (so `hasMore` is false once the next page would start beyond the 200 cap).
- **AC 11.3** [Anonymous] **Given** a group was not requested, or is not permitted, **then** that group is `null` in the response. `mine` is always `null` for anonymous callers and for authenticated callers whose app user id is unresolved; requesting `types=mine` anonymously returns **200** with every group `null` (not an error).
- **AC 11.4** [Anonymous] **Given** `q` contains no searchable word (for example only punctuation) or only English stop words, **then** the API returns **200** with empty groups, and a query made only of excluded words (`-foo`) matches nothing. In topic (category and subcategory) search a negated word (a `-` at the start of a word) is skipped, so an exclusion never adds a topic match. Quotes (`"exact phrase"`), `or`, and `-exclude` are honoured. The last word, while it is still being typed (no trailing space, not inside quotes, not negated), also matches as a prefix. Queries are limited to 8 words of at most 40 characters each. Search syntax characters such as `! | ( : '` and non-ASCII text (for example `café`, `日本`, `½`, an emoji) never cause an error. If PostgreSQL still rejects a query the API returns **200** with empty groups and logs a warning that does not contain `q`; it never returns 500.
- **AC 11.5** [Anonymous] **Given** topic results, **then** `categories` and `subcategories` cover every taxonomy node (12 categories, L1 and L2 nodes) with `itemCount` equal to the number of **public** items under that node (private items are never counted, whoever is asking). Slugs repeat across categories (`general`), so a subcategory is identified by `categorySlug`, `l1` and `l2` together. Ranking: match quality (exact word, then prefix, then one typo; slug/alias matches above description matches), then category before L1 before L2, then nodes with items first, then more items, then slug.
- **AC 11.6** [Anonymous] **Given** collection results, **then** only collections that are public **and** discoverable are searched (a public collection that is not discoverable is not returned), ranked by relevance plus a small boost for item count. `itemCount` counts only non-private items. When I am signed in, my own collections are left out of this group because they appear in `mine`.
- **AC 11.7** [Anonymous] **Given** question results, **then** only items with `IsPrivate = false` are searched; being in a public collection does not make another user's private item discoverable, and admins get no extra visibility. Matching uses Question, CorrectAnswer and Explanation (weights A, B, C). Each result carries `snippet.text` (the Question only, plain text) and `snippet.highlights` (`{ start, length }` ranges). Offsets are UTF-16 code unit indices, the text never contains highlight markers, and CorrectAnswer and Explanation are never returned.
- **AC 11.8** [Anonymous] **Given** an item result, **then** `categorySlug`, `l1` and `l2` are returned only for a non-private category and non-private navigation keywords; otherwise those fields are `null`. No emails, `CreatedBy` values or other users' identifiers are returned.
- **AC 11.9** [Authenticated] **Given** I am signed in, **then** `mine` searches only my own: collections I created (any visibility), my unexpired study guide documents (`ExpiresUtc` in the future, title match), and my private items. Each result is `{ kind, id, title }` with `kind` one of `collection`, `studyGuideDocument`, `item`. I never see another user's private items, collections or study guide documents, and an expired document is not returned even if it is mine.
- **AC 11.10** [Anonymous] **Given** a caller makes more than 120 `GET /search` requests in one minute, **then** the API returns **429** with a `Retry-After` header and `detail` "Too many searches. Please wait a moment and try again." The window is a fixed one-minute window partitioned by user subject when authenticated, otherwise by the server-trusted client IP (`Connection.RemoteIpAddress`), exactly as in AC 5.6.4: a client-sent `X-Forwarded-For` value never selects the partition, so rotating it buys no budget. The production `ForwardedHeaders:KnownNetworks` value is verified before deploy.
- **AC 11.11** [System] **Given** the OpenAPI artifact is regenerated, **then** `GET /search` appears under the `Search` tag with 200, 400 and 429 responses.
- **AC 11.12** [System] **Given** the topic counts are older than 10 minutes, **when** the next search asks for topics, **then** the counts are reloaded (public items only); a failed reload keeps the previous counts and retries after a minute.
- **AC 11.13** [System] **Given** the page-view and analytics APIs, **then** the text a visitor searched for is never accepted or stored: `/search` page views lose their query string (AC 5.3.21) and the search analytics events have no field for it (AC 5.4.11).
- **AC 11.14** [System] **Given** ASP.NET Core logs a request for `/search` (for example "Request starting ... /search?q=..." or "Request finished"), **then** the logged `QueryString` is replaced with `?[redacted]`, so the search text does not reach the console or Loki sinks. The API itself never logs `q`.

**UI (header search and the `/search` page, #321)**

- **AC 11.15** [Anyone] **Given** the header, **then** exactly one search variant renders at a time (chosen with a media query, so ids never duplicate): at `xl` (1280 px) and up an inline input (224 px wide, `role="search"` named "Site search", with a visible shortcut hint while empty and unfocused ("⌘K" on Apple platforms, "Ctrl K" elsewhere)); below `xl` a magnifier icon button (`aria-label` "Search") that opens the full-screen overlay. No navigation link is hidden or wrapped at 1024 px or 1280 px. The dropdown sits above page content (the header has its own stacking level). There is no popover at 1024-1279 px.
- **AC 11.16** [Anyone] **Given** I open the overlay (icon or shortcut), **then** a modal dialog named "Search" fills the screen, the input is focused (text 16 px or larger, so iOS does not zoom), the page behind does not scroll, Tab cycles between the input and Cancel only, and the page has no horizontal scroll at 375 px. **When** I press Escape or Cancel, **then** it closes and focus returns to the icon. Choosing a result, or any route change, closes it without moving focus back; crossing into `xl` swaps to the inline input and keeps the text.
- **AC 11.17** [Anyone] **Given** I press Ctrl+K (or Cmd+K), **then** search opens or focuses with any text selected, even from inside another input, and the browser default is prevented, **unless** another dialog (`role="dialog"` or `aria-modal`) is mounted. `/` does the same but follows the AC 3.16.5 ignore rules (inputs, textareas, selects, contentEditable, textbox/combobox roles, IME composition, auto-repeat, already-handled keys, Ctrl/Alt/Meta held, a mounted dialog); the predicate is shared with the study hotkeys.
- **AC 11.18** [Anyone] **Given** I type 2 or more characters, **then** after a 200 ms pause one `GET /search` runs (earlier in-flight requests are cancelled, nothing is sent per keystroke, nothing is sent below 2 characters) with `limit=5`, and the dropdown lists groups Topics, Collections, Questions and (signed in) Mine, empty groups omitted, at most 5 rows each; Topics merges categories and subcategories (subcategories shown as `tech › ai-ml` with the slugs highlighted, description and public item count beside them). A group with more shows a "See all ..." row that opens `/search?q=...&tab=...`; a final "See all results for “...”" row opens `/search?q=...`. Question rows show the server snippet with highlights (rendered as text only, never as HTML) and `category › l1 › l2`. Titles are highlighted from the typed words on the client.
- **AC 11.19** [Anyone] **Given** the dropdown is open, **then** DOM focus stays in the input and the first row becomes active once results for the current text arrive; ArrowDown/ArrowUp move through rows (headings skipped) and wrap; Alt+ArrowDown or ArrowDown on a closed panel opens it; Enter opens the active row; Escape closes the panel and keeps the text, and a second Escape clears the input; Tab closes the panel and moves on. **When** results shown belong to earlier text (still debouncing or loading), **then** nothing is active and Enter searches the text as typed at `/search?q=...`; Enter with no active row does the same. Rows ignore mouse-down so the input keeps focus.
- **AC 11.20** [Anyone] **Given** the input, **then** it is a `role="combobox"` with `aria-autocomplete="list"`, `aria-keyshortcuts="Control+K Meta+K"` and a screen-reader label "Search Quizymode"; `aria-expanded` is true only while a listbox with at least one option is shown and `aria-controls` and `aria-activedescendant` are set only then; the listbox (`role="listbox"`, groups labelled by their headings, options with unique ids and `aria-selected`) is not rendered for hint, empty, loading or rate-limited states; a persistent polite live region announces "N results available. Use up and down arrow keys to navigate.", "No results for ..." and the error. axe reports no WCAG 2 A/AA/2.1 AA violations in the open dropdown.
- **AC 11.21** [Anyone] **Given** the dropdown, **then** with an empty input it shows "Search topics, collections and questions."; with one character "Keep typing. Search needs at least 2 characters." and sends nothing; while the first request is in flight three skeleton rows (panel `aria-busy`); while a newer request runs the previous rows stay at reduced opacity with a spinner in the input; with no results "No results for “...”", "Check the spelling or try fewer words." and a "Browse all categories" link (no listbox); on a non-429 failure "Could not load search results." plus a "Try again" option reachable with ArrowDown and Enter (never auto-activated, never retried automatically); on **429** a static amber notice "You are searching too fast. Try again in N seconds." (N from `Retry-After`, otherwise "Please wait a few seconds and try again.") with a manual "Try again" button; there is no automatic retry and no request is made until it is used.
- **AC 11.22** [Anyone] **Given** I am anonymous, **then** the UI never requests `mine`, there is no Mine group or tab, and `tab=mine` on `/search` is treated as All. **Given** I am signed in, **then** the dropdown and the All tab request `mine` and show my collections, study guide documents and private questions (labelled Collection, Study guide, Question), linking to `/collections/:id/:slug`, `/study-guides/documents/:id` and `/items/:id`. A null `mine` group counts as empty.
- **AC 11.23** [Anyone] **Given** I open `/search?q=&tab=all|topics|collections|questions|mine&page=`, **then** the URL is the only source of truth (an invalid tab becomes All; `page` is 1-11 and ignored on All; `q` is capped at 100 characters), the page is `noindex` with title "Search" (the text never appears in the title), the heading is `Results for “...”` (or "Search"), a search form (`role="search"`, "Search Quizymode") is prefilled and submitting keeps the tab and resets the page. A tab list (All, Topics, Collections, Questions, and Mine when signed in; no counts) uses `role="tablist"`/`tab`/`tabpanel`, a roving tabindex, ArrowLeft/ArrowRight (wrapping), Home and End; tab and page changes replace the history entry. With fewer than 2 characters it shows "Type at least 2 characters to search topics, collections and questions." and sends nothing. The page lazy-loads (AC 8.2). axe reports no violations on any tab and there is no horizontal scroll at 375 px.
- **AC 11.24** [Anyone] **Given** the All tab, **then** it requests `limit=5` and shows each non-empty group as a section with a heading and a "See all ..." button that switches tab. **Given** a single-type tab, **then** it requests `limit=20` and `offset=(page-1)*20`, rows are real links, Previous is disabled on page 1, Next is enabled only when the group reports `hasMore` (so it is disabled at the 220-result cap), the previous rows stay at reduced opacity while the next page loads, and after a page change focus moves to a hidden heading such as "Topics, page 2". Topics shows categories and subcategories together, so a Topics page can hold up to 40 rows. With no results: All shows a dashed card with "Browse categories" and "Browse collections" links; a single-type tab shows "No ... for “...”" with a "Search everything" button. Errors and 429 use the same copy and manual retry as AC 11.21.
- **AC 11.25** [Anyone] **Given** a result, **then** it links to: category `/categories/{slug}`; subcategory `/categories/{category}/{l1}` or `/categories/{category}/{l1}/{l2}`; collection `/collections/{id}/{name-slug}`; question `/items/{id}`; my study guide document `/study-guides/documents/{id}`.
- **AC 11.26** [System] **Given** analytics, **then** `search_performed` fires once per committed search with `total` (the number of results shown, zero allowed) and context type `search`: when a result is chosen from the dropdown, and when `/search` finishes loading results for a new text (not per keystroke, not on tab or page changes, and not again for the dropdown "See all"/Enter paths, which land on `/search`). `search_result_clicked` fires on choosing a result with its 1-based `position` and a context: `category` with the category slug, `collection` with the collection id, `item` with the item id, or `mine` with no id for the caller's own content. No payload, URL path or query string sent to the analytics APIs contains the search text (AC 5.3.21, 5.4.11).
- **AC 11.27** [Admin] **Given** I open Usage Analytics, **then** the new `search` feature area is labelled "Search" in the area tables.
- **AC 11.28** [Anyone] **Given** I use search in the web app, **then** cached search results are keyed per user (anonymous visitors share one key), so another account on the same tab never sees my `mine` titles, and all cached search results are purged when I sign out.

---

## Unclarities / questions for product owner

- **Shareable link vs IsPublic**: Resolved — if a collection is **not** shared with others (IsPublic = false), only the owner can access it by ID or link. When **Shared with others** is on (IsPublic = true), anyone with the link (or who finds it by ID or via Discover) can view and quiz, and the collection appears in Discover. The UI uses the label **"Shared with others"** for this toggle. Users can search or enter a collection ID to open it (see AC 1.9.6).
- **Default collection**: Resolved — at signup the backend creates a personalized default collection when the user's display name is available: name = first 3 characters + `'s Collection` and description = `<display name> default collection` (for example `Abc's Collection` / `Abcdefgh default collection`). If a user has zero collections (e.g. legacy), the first `GET /collections` creates the same shape. If no display name is available, it falls back to "Default Collection".
- **Private keyword name collision**: When a public keyword and a private keyword share the same name/slug, navigation and item filtering resolve that name/slug to **one effective keyword** per scope: the **public** keyword is used when it exists; if no public keyword exists with that name/slug, the user's private keyword is used instead. This ensures a single, predictable path/keyword resolution when names collide.
- **Cloze sibling leak and grammar (#261)**: Cards created from one cloze line are saved as independent items with no link between them, so quiz and flashcard sessions can show a sibling's answer while another card from the same line is asked; the review step warns about this and the user may reject siblings. Distractors are written by the user, so in inflected languages they must already be in the grammatical form that fits the sentence; the app does not inflect them. With a multi-blank card (same cN used more than once) the correct answer is the answers joined with ", " (for example "H, O"), so typed-answer mode (#256) requires that joined text exactly.

---

## Updating this document

When you change application behavior (API contracts, authorization rules, or user-visible flows), update this document so it stays accurate.

- **API vs UI:** Keep **API** (HTTP, status codes, payloads, authorization) and **UI** (labels, screens, controls) in separate subsections under each feature so backend and frontend concerns stay separate.
- **Actors:** Use the standard actors: **Owner**, **Authenticated (non-owner)**, **Admin (non-owner)**, **Anonymous**. State the actor in each AC as `[Actor]`. For Admin (non-owner), use "Same as Authenticated (non-owner) (AC X.Y.Z)" when behavior is the same.
- **Format:** Use **AC section.subsection.id** with Given/When/Then; reference endpoints and status codes in API criteria, and copy/controls in UI criteria.
- **Atomicity:** Aim for **one AC = one verifiable behavior**. Avoid mixing concerns (e.g. do not combine route behavior, API response shape, UI labels, navigation, and error handling in a single AC); instead, split into separate ACs that each test one thing.
- See the Cursor rule "Update acceptance criteria when app behavior changes" for automation hints.
